				<h2>Current Vacancies</h2>
				<p>This listing includes all vacancies open to the general public.  To view a vacancy's details, click on the title.</p>
				<p>To sort the listing by the values of a column, click on the column heading to sort in ascending order.  Click the column heading again to sort in descending order.  All columns are sortable.  The search box will filter the listings based on what you enter.</p>
